At scale $\varepsilon$, there is no universal comparison between the three learning tasks: both $m_{CoT}/m_{base}$ and $m_{e2e}/m_{CoT}$ can be made simultaneously arbitrarily larger than $M/\varepsilon$, the natural analogue for the existing deterministic results. Nevertheless, after altering scales, for every class, CoT learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded by base learning at scale $\varepsilon/M^2$, whereas e2e learning at scale $\varepsilon$ is upper-bounded, up to logarithmic factors, by $(M/\varepsilon) m_{CoT}(Θ(\varepsilon))$. These dependencies and scales are essentially tight. We complement these bounds by studying dimension $d$ logistic functions in our model.

</details>


### [143] [Learning Suffers More Than the Policy Class Under Partial Observability: A Closed-Form Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07228)
*Idil Gözel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测的线性二次控制问题，发现即使最优策略可表示且价值函数足够表达，标准actor-critic学习器仍会收敛到远非最优的策略。原因是critic存在偏差——agent无法将观测归因于不可观测的状态部分，导致critic将未解释的变化误读为自身价值估计的锐利曲率，actor随之偏离最优。通过闭式解推导出策略、代价及消除该问题的设计选择（前瞻范围），深度强化学习实验验证了这些预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的强化学习场景中，以往研究主要归咎于策略表达能力不足——认为agent看不到足够的状态所以无法表示好策略。作者质疑这一普遍认知，试图证明即使好策略可表示且价值函数足够表达，学习过程本身仍可能失败，根本原因可能在学习器的偏差而非表达限制。

Method: 作者研究了一个部分可观测的线性二次问题（partially observed LQ problem），在其中标准actor-critic学习器可以被闭式求解。通过数学推导分析critic学习偏差的产生机制，给出策略、代价以及前瞻范围（lookahead horizon）这一设计选择的闭式表达式，并用深度强化学习实验验证理论预测。

Result: 默认设置下，agent可表示的最优策略成本仅比全观测理想控制器高10.4%，但学习算法却收敛到比最佳可用策略差35%的策略。研究发现根因是critic将不可观测状态引起的方差误读为价值函数的锐利曲率，actor跟随该错误偏离最优。改变学习器的前瞻范围可以消除该问题，而给agent提供过去观测的记忆却无效。深度RL实验结果与理论预测高度吻合。

Conclusion: 部分可观测RL的性能瓶颈可能不在策略表达能力的上限，而在于critic学习中的系统性偏差。即使最优策略可表示，学习过程本身也可能因为无法正确解析状态归因而陷入次优。这一发现提示，在设计部分可观测RL系统时，调整前瞻范围比增加记忆更有效，且需要从学习偏差的角度重新审视失败原因。

Abstract: When a reinforcement learning agent cannot observe the full state, we usually blame its policies: it cannot see enough to represent a good one. We show that in a solvable case the bigger problem lies elsewhere. Even when a good policy is available and the agent's value function is expressive enough to describe it exactly, learning still ends up somewhere far worse.
  We study a partially observed linear-quadratic problem in which a standard actor-critic learner can be solved in closed form. At our default setting the best policy the agent can represent is already close to optimal, costing 10.4% more than the ideal controller that observes everything. Learning does not find it. The algorithm instead comes to rest at a policy that is 35% worse than the best one available to it, and we can say exactly where and why.
  The cause is a bias in what the critic learns rather than a limit on what the actor can express. Because the agent cannot attribute what it sees to the part of the state it cannot observe, the critic misreads that unexplained variation as sharp curvature in its own value estimates, and the actor follows that error away from the optimum. We derive closed-form expressions for the resulting policy, for its cost, and for the one design choice that removes the problem, which is how far the learner looks ahead before trusting its own value estimates. Deep reinforcement learning experiments follow these predictions closely. Notably, giving the agent memory of past observations does not help, while changing how far it looks ahead does.

</details>


### [144] [$\boldsymbolλ$-Orthogonality Regularization for Compatible Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2509.16664)
*Simone Ricci,Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Ioannis Patras,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出λ-正交正则化方法，在仿射变换上施加松弛的正交约束，在实现分布适配的同时保留原始表示空间的结构，确保跨模型更新的兼容性并保持零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索系统依赖强大模型学习到的表示，但训练成本高且表示不一致。现有两种适配方法各有缺陷：仿射变换适应特定分布好但会显著改变原始表示；正交变换能保持原始结构但有严格几何约束限制适应性。需要在更新模型与先前模型的潜在空间之间适配下游分布，同时保留新学习到的表示空间。

Method: 在仿射变换过程中施加λ-正交正则化（λ-Orthogonality regularization），即在正交约束和完全松弛之间通过参数λ进行调节，从而既能获得分布特定的适配能力，又能保留原始学习到的表示结构。

Result: 在多种架构和数据集上的大量实验验证了该方法，证明其能够保持模型的零样本性能并确保跨模型更新的兼容性。

Conclusion: λ-正交正则化为仿射变换与正交约束之间提供了平衡点，同时兼顾了分布适配性和表示结构保留，有效解决了跨模型表示兼容性问题。

Abstract: Retrieval systems rely on representations learned by increasingly powerful models. However, due to the high training cost and inconsistencies in learned representations, there is significant interest in facilitating communication between representations and ensuring compatibility across independently trained neural networks. In the literature, two primary approaches are commonly used to adapt different learned representations: affine transformations, which adapt well to specific distributions but can significantly alter the original representation, and orthogonal transformations, which preserve the original structure with strict geometric constraints but limit adaptability. A key challenge is adapting the latent spaces of updated models to align with those of previous models on downstream distributions while preserving the newly learned representation spaces. In this paper, we impose a relaxed orthogonality constraint, namely $λ$-Orthogonality regularization, while learning an affine transformation, to obtain distribution-specific adaptation while retaining the original learned representations. Extensive experiments across various architectures and datasets validate our approach, demonstrating that it preserves the model's zero-shot performance and ensures compatibility across model updates. Code available at: \href{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda_orthogonality.git}{https://github.com/miccunifi/lambda\_orthogonality}.

</details>


### [145] [TOFD: Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling against Poisoning Attacks in Split Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07274)
*Yuhan Xie,Jingrong Huang,Chen Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TOFD（面向目标的特征解耦）框架，这是一个针对分割联邦学习（SFL）中投毒攻击的统一防御框架，通过目标推断、样本净化和解耦优化三阶段实现主动检测与鲁棒优化，在五个数据集上显著优于现有防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFL虽通过拆分架构降低了客户端计算开销并保护隐私，但其独特的拆分架构引入了新的攻击面，容易遭受多种投毒攻击。现有防御方法未能利用分割范式的特性，难以在早期阶段检测和遏制恶意行为，因此亟需一个统一的防御框架。

Method: TOFD包含三个阶段：(1) 目标推断——通过类别特定Margin Perturbation（MP）精炼类别安全区域来识别潜在攻击目标；(2) 样本净化——利用跨类别min-max归一化MP校准的阈值自适应过滤投毒的smashed data；(3) 解耦优化——借助对抗引导模型捕获攻击诱导模式，并在优化过程中解耦其影响以抑制残余对抗效应。论文还提供了TOFD收敛性的理论保证。

Result: 在五个数据集上的大量实验表明，TOFD在多种攻击场景下一致优于现有最先进的防御方法，实现了卓越的鲁棒性，同时计算开销低，适合实际部署。

Conclusion: TOFD通过充分利用分割联邦学习的范式特性，将主动检测与鲁棒优化统一到一个框架中，有效解决了SFL面临的多样化投毒攻击威胁，为分割联邦学习的安全防护提供了一种高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Split Federated Learning (SFL) facilitates privacy-preserving collaborative training with reduced client-side overhead. However, its split architecture introduces unique attack surfaces, rendering it vulnerable to diverse poisoning attacks. Most existing defenses fail to exploit the split paradigm, limiting their ability to detect and contain malicious behaviors at an early stage. To bridge this gap, we propose Target-Oriented Feature Decoupling (TOFD), a unified framework that jointly enables proactive detection and robust optimization against a wide range of poisoning attacks. TOFD operates in three stages: (1) Target Inference, which identifies potential attack targets by refining class-wise safe zones via class-specific Margin Perturbation (MP); (2) Sample Purification, which adaptively filters poisoned smashed data using thresholds calibrated through cross-class min-max normalization of MP; and (3) Decoupling Optimization, which leverages an adversarial guidance model to capture attack-induced patterns and decouple their influence during optimization, thereby suppressing residual adversarial effects. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of TOFD. Extensive experiments on five datasets demonstrate that TOFD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art defenses under diverse attack scenarios, achieving superior robustness with low computational overhead suitable for practical deployment.

</details>


### [146] [A foundation-model approach to pediatric headache classification from rs-fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07287)
*Guilherme S. Imai Aldeia,Clara Moon,Julie Shulman,Navil Sethna,Allison Smith,Alyssa Lebel,William G. La Cava,Scott Holmes*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用NeuroSTORM基础模型对儿童rs-fMRI数据进行编码和微调，用于头痛分类，在区分头痛与非头痛任务中达到AUROC 0.82，优于基于功能连接矩阵的传统方法（AUROC 0.67）。尽管在头痛亚型细分类别中表现有限（macro-AUROC 0.69），但该研究表明NeuroSTORM能在有限数据条件下捕捉与头痛相关的潜在rs-fMRI表征，为fMRI预测儿童头痛提供了概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 头痛是儿童中最常见的神经系统疾病，严重影响生活质量。当前缺乏利用静息态功能磁共振成像（rs-fMRI）结合机器学习对儿童头痛进行分类的研究，尤其是在有限数据条件下探索更优的表征学习方法。

Method: 使用NeuroSTORM（近期提出的基础模型）对rs-fMRI数据进行编码并微调，区分健康对照与头痛患儿，进而对头痛亚型分类。将NeuroSTORM与基于功能连接（FC）矩阵的标准神经科学方法进行对比。数据来自189次rs-fMRI扫描、110名个体的两轮访问，头痛患病率为74%。采用AUROC、AUPRC和macro-AUROC作为评估指标。

Result: NeuroSTORM在区分头痛与非头痛任务中达到AUROC 0.82（95% CI, 0.82-0.82）和AUPRC 0.93（95% CI, 0.93-0.94），显著优于FC矩阵方法的AUROC 0.67（95% CI, 0.67-0.67）和AUPRC 0.85。在三分类任务（健康对照、慢性偏头痛、非慢性头痛）中，NeuroSTORM达到macro-AUROC 0.69（95% CI, 0.68-0.69），能够区分慢性偏头痛，但难以区分其他头痛亚型与慢性偏头痛。

Conclusion: 研究表明NeuroSTORM在有限数据条件下无需依赖FC特征即可捕捉到可迁移至头痛相关任务的潜在rs-fMRI表征。该工作为基于fMRI的儿童头痛预测提供了概念验证，并显示了未来在头痛亚型识别和个体化治疗策略方面的潜在应用价值。

Abstract: Headache is the most common neurological disorder in children and substantially affects quality of life. We investigated whether resting-state functional MRI (rs-fMRI) can support pediatric headache classification using machine learning. We encoded rs-fMRI data using NeuroSTORM, a recent foundation model, and fine-tuned it to distinguish healthy controls from children with headache and subsequently classify headache subtypes. We compared NeuroSTORM with a standard neuroscience approach using functional-connectivity (FC) matrices derived from brain activity as predictors. Using 189 rs-fMRI scans from 110 individuals collected across two visits (prevalence of any headache: 74%), NeuroSTORM achieved an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.82 (95% CI, 0.82-0.82) and an area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) of 0.93 (95% CI, 0.93-0.94) for discriminating headache from non-headache. In contrast, models trained on FC matrices showed lower performance (AUROC, 0.67 [95% CI, 0.67-0.67]; AUPRC, 0.85 [95% CI, 0.85-0.85]). In multiclass classification of healthy controls, chronic migraine, and non-chronic headaches (e.g., post-viral headache, new daily persistent headache, post-traumatic headache), NeuroSTORM achieved a macro-AUROC of 0.69 (95% CI, 0.68-0.69). Results suggest that the approach can distinguish chronic migraine but has difficulty differentiating other headache subtypes from chronic migraine. Overall, under limited-data conditions, NeuroSTORM appears to capture latent rs-fMRI representations that transfer to headache-related tasks without relying on FC features. These findings provide proof of concept for fMRI-based prediction of pediatric headache and highlight potential future utility for subtype identification and individualized treatment strategies.

</details>


### [147] [FUSE: Feature-Wise Unified Specialization with Cross-Column Exchange for Mixed-Type Tabular Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07294)
*Suman Cha,Seongchan Lee,Dohyun Ko,Hyunjoong Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FUSE框架，通过特征级统一特化与跨列信息交换机制，将数值与类别特征的独立处理和跨列依赖建模显式分离，用于混合型表格数据的生成。实验在8个数据集上验证了其在分布保真度和下游效用指标上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分流匹配虽能通过因子化分布处理不同类型的端点特征，但其特征特定的处理和跨列交互仍隐式地共享于同一骨干网络中，缺乏显式的分工，这限制了混合型表格数据生成的质量。

Method: FUSE框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 分别针对数值和类别特征应用独立的自适应混合模块，使每个特征能够组合共享的特化子网络；(2) 通过联合注意力机制在所有列之间保持信息交换。此外，论文还从理论上刻画了受限条件上下文下的超额总体风险，并通过端点预测风险界定了连续Wasserstein生成误差。

Result: 在8个表格数据集上的综合实验表明，FUSE在分布保真度和下游效用指标上均取得了强劲且一致的表现。

Conclusion: FUSE通过显式分离特征特定处理与跨列交互，为混合型表格数据生成提供了有效的建模方案，同时理论分析为限制条件上下文下的生成误差提供了保障。

Abstract: Generating mixed-type tabular data requires jointly modeling diverse feature distributions and their complex cross-column dependencies. Variational flow matching handles distinct endpoints via factorized distributions, yet leaves feature-specific processing and cross-column interactions implicit within a shared backbone. We introduce Feature-wise Unified Specialization with cross-column Exchange (FUSE) to explicitly separate these roles. FUSE applies separate adaptive mixture modules to numerical and categorical features, allowing each feature to combine shared specialized subnetworks, while joint attention preserves information exchange across all columns. We also characterize the excess population risk from restricted conditioning contexts and bound the continuous Wasserstein generation error by endpoint-prediction risk. Comprehensive experiments on eight tabular datasets demonstrate that FUSE achieves strong and consistent performance across distributional fidelity and downstream utility metrics.

</details>


### [148] [From Optimal Actions to World Models: Identifiability of Transition Kernels in Discounted MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07301)
*Neal Batra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了仅从最优动作（而非数值Q值）出发，能恢复马尔可夫决策过程中转移概率的何种信息。对于状态-动作奖励r(s,a)，两个转移核给出的最优动作集对每个奖励都相同的充要条件是一个关于核P和可逆矩阵L的等式，且在某核附近存在n(n-1)维的核族。对于依赖下一状态的奖励r(s,a,s')，通常可以恢复转移核本身；而状态奖励r(s)则能恢复的信息更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆向强化学习/逆向动力学问题中，一个核心问题是：仅从最优行为（而非数值Q值）出发，能否恢复马尔可夫决策过程的动力系统（转移概率）？此前Letcher等人研究了从数值Q值恢复动力学的问题，但数值本身往往不可观测，实际中我们只能观测到最优动作。研究奖励函数的形式如何影响从最优动作中可恢复的动力学信息，是理解逆向问题可识别性的关键。

Method: 作者对三类奖励形式分别进行分析：（1）状态-动作奖励r(s,a)：推导出两个转移核给出相同最优动作的充要条件为Q_{s,a}=(P_{s,a}+(1/γ)e_s^T(L-I))L^{-1}，其中L为满足L1=1的可逆矩阵；并证明在严格正核附近存在n(n-1)维的核族具有此性质，即便限制在每状态唯一最优动作的奖励上也成立。（2）状态-动作-下一状态奖励r(s,a,s')：通常可完全确定转移核，每个至少有两个动作的状态的行都被决定，并精确刻画了单动作状态行可隐藏的条件。（3）状态奖励r(s)：两个核给出相同最优动作当且仅当每个确定性策略对同一组奖励都是最优的。

Result: 核心结果为：（1）对于r(s,a)奖励，核的非唯一性由n(n-1)维自由度刻画，即存在一族不同核产生完全相同的最优动作集合；（2）对于r(s,a,s')奖励，转移核几乎完全可以恢复，仅单动作状态的行存在隐藏可能；（3）对于r(s)奖励，可恢复的信息最为有限。不同奖励形式对动力学可恢复性有本质性影响。

Conclusion: 奖励函数的形式从根本上决定了从最优动作中能恢复多少关于转移概率的信息。当奖励依赖下一状态时，动力学几乎完全可识别；当奖励仅为状态-动作时，存在一个n(n-1)维的不可识别核族；当奖励仅为状态时，恢复能力最弱。这些结果对逆向强化学习和逆动力学问题的可识别性边界提供了精确刻画。

Abstract: We study what can be recovered about the transition probabilities of a Markov decision process from optimal actions alone. This is closely related to the inverse problem considered by Letcher et al., who ask when the dynamics can be recovered from numerical \(Q\)-values. Here the numerical values themselves are not observed; only the optimal actions are known, for every reward in a given class.
  For state-action rewards \(r(s,a)\), knowing the optimal actions for every reward also tells us how much better one action is than another when each is followed by the same fixed policy. This is still not enough to determine the transition probabilities uniquely. We prove that two kernels give the same optimal actions for every reward exactly when \[ Q_{s,a} =
\Bigl(P_{s,a}+\tfrac1γe_s^{\mathsf T}(L-I)\Bigr)L^{-1} \] for one invertible matrix \(L\) satisfying \(L\mathbf 1=\mathbf 1\). Near a kernel with strictly positive entries, there is an \(n(n-1)\)-dimensional family of different kernels with this property. The result is unchanged if we consider only rewards having a unique optimal action at every state.
  We then compare this with rewards of the forms \(r(s)\) and \(r(s,a,s')\). Rewards that depend on the next state can usually recover the transition kernel itself: every row at a state with at least two actions is determined, and we describe exactly when a row at a state with one action can remain hidden. State rewards reveal less: two kernels give the same optimal actions exactly when every deterministic policy is optimal for the same set of rewards. The results show how the form of the reward affects what can be learned about the dynamics from optimal actions alone.

</details>


### [149] [Is SwiGLU's Open Positive Tail Necessary? Evidence from Closed-Tail Gating with MemGLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07323)
*Yuting Ge,Pengju Yang,Mingkai Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文测试解码器-only语言模型FFN是否依赖SwiGLU的开放正尾。提出的MemGLU（基于忆阻分支几何的闭尾比较器）在9M和30M规模配对预训练中，验证NLL与SwiGLU差距约0.1%。实验表明开放正尾并非解码器-only LLM FFN的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SwiGLU是当前LLM中广泛使用的激活函数，其开放正尾被认为是其性能优势的关键。作者希望探究这一设计选择是否真正必要，通过构造一个闭合正尾的对照激活函数（MemGLU）来系统测试其必要性。

Method: 作者从忆阻分支几何出发设计了MemGLU，其具有闭合正尾，作为SwiGLU的对照。在9M和30M参数规模下进行配对预训练，每组使用三个随机种子以保证统计可靠性。训练后对SwiGLU检查点施加正尾抑制以测试敏感性，并通过机制诊断分析两个模型的门控使用差异。

Result: MemGLU在验证NLL上与SwiGLU性能差距保持在约0.1%以内。已训练的SwiGLU检查点在正尾被抑制时表现敏感；但机制诊断显示两种模型虽然损失相近，门控使用方式却存在差异，说明模型会适应预训练时提供的门几何形状。

Conclusion: 在测试的规模范围内，SwiGLU的开放正尾对于解码器-only语言模型FFN并非必要。模型能够根据预训练阶段可用的门激活几何进行适应性调整，这一发现对激活函数设计和模型架构选择具有启示意义。

Abstract: We test whether decoder-only language-model FFNs require SwiGLU's open positive tail. We introduce MemGLU as a closed-tail comparator derived from a memristive branch geometry. Across paired 9M and 30M pretraining runs with three seeds, MemGLU remains within about 0.1% of SwiGLU in validation NLL. Trained SwiGLU checkpoints are sensitive to positive-tail suppression, while mechanism diagnostics show that the two models use their gates differently despite similar losses. These results suggest that models adapt to the gate geometry available during pretraining. At the tested scales, SwiGLU's open positive tail is not necessary for decoder-only language-model FFNs.

</details>


### [150] [When GNNs Fail: Quantifying and Overcoming Temporal Correlation Volatility in Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07333)
*Chen Shao,Yue Wang,Zhenyi Zhu,Zhanbo Huang,Tobias Käfer,Zonghan Wu,Danai Koutra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TCV指标量化多元时间序列中变量间动态相关性的变化程度，揭示现有GNN和Transformer模型在高TCV场景下的性能退化问题，并提出GLIDE图神经网络层，通过路径消息传递和动静传播分离两个理论驱动机制，在静态和动态场景下平均性能提升最高达45.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于GNN的多变量时间序列预测方法假设静态图拓扑，忽略变量间相关性随时间的剧烈变化。作者希望形式化地量化这种动态演化并揭示当前架构的局限性，从而推动更鲁棒的预测模型设计。

Method: 1) 提出Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV)，一种模型无关指标，用于量化潜在图结构的分布演化程度；2) 建立TCV与模型性能退化之间的明确联系；3) 提出GLIDE (Graph Layer for Inference in Dynamic Environments)，包含两个理论驱动的设计机制：D1路径消息传递（Path-based Message Passing）捕获路径级邻域信息，D2静态与动态传播分离（Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation）通过局部静态近似识别最优动态。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准上的大量实验表明，GLIDE在静态和动态设置下平均性能提升最高达45.6%，最大单项提升可达85.7%。相比Transformer等流行模型，在高TCV环境中GLIDE显著更加鲁棒。

Conclusion: GLIDE通过在动态拓扑条件下同时保持静态场景的鲁棒性，有效解决了现有GNN架构在面对时间变化的相关结构时的表示能力瓶颈，为动态图时间序列预测提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Modeling multivariate time series by representing them as graphs, where individual series act as nodes and pairwise temporal corre- lations serve as edges, has gained significant traction. Recent advances in Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have demonstrated strong perfor- mance by assuming a static graph topology and aggregating information from neighboring series. In this work, we investigate the representa- tional power of GNNs for forecasting under both static and dynamic settings (i.e., when pairwise correlations evolve drastically over time) and identify critical limitations in current architectures. To formalize this, we first propose Temporal Correlation Volatility (TCV), a model- agnostic metric designed to quantify the distributional evolution of these latent structures. We establish a clear connection between TCV and performance degradation, demonstrating that many popular models, including Transformers, generalize poorly in high-TCV settings and are often outperformed by simple structure-agnostic baselines. To address these limitations, we propose Graph Layer for Inference in Dynamic En- vironments (GLIDE), a novel GNN layer enhanced by two theoretically grounded design mechanisms: (D1) Path-based Message Passing, which captures path-based neighborhoods and (D2) Static and Dynamic Propagation Separation, which identifies optimal dynamics via local static approximation. These components significantly improve learning under dynamic topology while preserving robustness in static scenarios. Ex- tensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GLIDE improves average performance by up to 45.6% across static and dynamic settings, with the largest gain reaching 85.7%. The source code is available at https://github.com/ChenS676/GLIDE.

</details>


### [151] [Aftab: A Comprehensive Benchmark of CNN Encoders and Advanced Value Functions in Parallelized Q-Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07335)
*Taha Shieenavaz,Shabnam Zareshahraki,Loris Nanni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了在无回放缓冲区、并行化深度强化学习范式（PQN）下卷积神经网络视觉编码器的架构设计空间，提出了复合架构Aftab，在Atari-57和Procgen Hard基准上分别达到6.479的IQM人类归一化分数和0.418的IQM归一化分数，显著优于标准PQN基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习日益倾向简化的并行化范式（如PQN），其在无回放缓冲区条件下实现稳定的离策略学习。然而，此类缓冲区无关设定下视觉编码器的表示能力和参数效率尚未得到充分探索，需要系统性的架构设计研究。

Method: 系统设计并评估了八种不同的CNN拓扑结构，在严格参数约束下优化样本效率；同时整合Hadamax编码范式以及分布性（distributional）、集成（ensemble）和决斗（dueling）头等Q-learning增强技术，构建复合架构Aftab。

Result: 在Atari-57基准上，Aftab实现6.479的IQM人类归一化分数，较标准PQN基线有0.86的概率提升；在Procgen Hard上取得0.418的IQM归一化分数，优于基线0.382，验证了分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为无缓冲并行化模型无关强化学习建立了高效且概率上更优的结构参考，同时保持了无缓冲、并行化优化的简洁性和内存效率。

Abstract: Recent advancements in deep reinforcement learning have increasingly favored simplified, highly parallelized paradigms. Notably, the Parallelized Q-Network (PQN) algorithm achieves stable off-policy learning without relying on computationally expensive replay buffers or target networks. However, the representational capacity and parameter efficiency of visual encoders operating in these buffer-free settings remain underexplored. In this work, we systematically investigate the architectural design space of Convolutional Neural Networks for PQN. We design and rigorously evaluate eight distinct CNN topologies, optimizing for sample efficiency under strict parameter constraints. Furthermore, we study the impact of representation and value estimation enhancements by integrating the Hadamax encoding paradigm and advanced Q-learning extensions, including distributional, ensemble, and dueling heads. Extensive experiments on the Atari-57 benchmark demonstrate that our proposed composite architecture, Aftab, achieves an Interquartile Mean (IQM) Human-Normalized Score of 6.479, establishing a 0.86 Probability of Improvement over the standard PQN baseline. Additionally, structural resilience evaluations on the highly non-stationary Procgen Hard benchmark confirm out-of-distribution generalization, with Aftab yielding an IQM Procgen Normalized Score of 0.418 compared to the baseline's 0.382. Ultimately, this work establishes an efficient, probabilistically superior structural reference for model-free reinforcement learning, all while preserving the simplicity and memory efficiency of unbuffered, parallelized optimization.
  The complete Aftab framework, including all model definitions, training configurations, and raw experimental logs, is open-sourced and available on our GitHub repository: https://github.com/tahashieenavaz/aftab

</details>


### [152] [Residual Algebra for Representation-Preserving Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07349)
*Yao Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种代数化的残差学习方法（Fold + FPRC-PQ），将表示视为携带坐标系和残差的"类型化对象"，通过有序的算子组合（松弛-聚合-闭合）保留或擦除表示身份，在3.67M中国A股日频数据上显著提升收益（净收益13.52%→19.10%，Sharpe 1.42→2.09），并通过理论证明（控制变量接口、一阶耦合路径均值正交性）和消融实验验证其增益来源于残差所有权与组合的显式化而非特征或模型容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异构表示学习仅做特征拼接，丢失了"哪个表示产生了哪些残差"的信息。作者认为错误归属（residual ownership）是学习的核心信息，主张将残差显式地代数化，使表示不仅包含坐标系统还包含其未解决的残差，从而克服拼接方法的盲区。

Method: 提出两个核心构件：(1) Fold，将对象实现为10×10秩网格上的点时刻条件均值场；(2) FPRC-PQ，实现"松弛-聚合-闭合"代数——每个场用自己的坐标和残差单独修正（relax），修正后在场间固定均值处汇合（身份擦除边界），再对聚合体的新残差进行共享学习（close）。复合算子精确分解为表示、局部残差估计、残差之残差估计；另提出"反思性回响算子"（reflective rumination）通过唯一正交投影而非网格搜索固定增益。

Result: 在2023-2026年3.67M中国A股股票-日观测（冻结点时刻协议）上，基础代数将净成本收益从13.52%提升至19.10%，Sharpe从1.42升至2.09。对照实验（匹配容量、统一残差、无身份两阶段、仅成对控制）均落后，证明增益来源于残差所有权与显式组合，而非更多特征或模型容量。

Conclusion: 将残差所有权和组合显式化是利用异构表示的关键。该框架兼具实证收益与理论保证（总体方差缩减的控制变量界面、沿骨干扰动的重拟合给出第一阶耦合路径均值正交性），提示表示学习应回归到"谁错了、在哪个坐标系中错、如何组合修正"这一结构化视角。

Abstract: Learning from heterogeneous representations is usually reduced to feature concatenation, which erases which representation produced an error. We instead algebraize the residual: a representation is a typed object that owns both a coordinate system and the residual it leaves unresolved, and learning is an ordered composition of operators that preserve or deliberately erase that type. Fold realizes the objects as point-in-time conditional-mean fields on 10x10 rank grids. FPRC-PQ realizes the algebra as relax-aggregate-close: each field is relaxed by a correction fitted to its own residual in its own coordinates; corrected fields meet at a fixed mean that is the sole identity-erasure boundary; and a shared learner closes only the aggregate's fresh residual. The composition telescopes exactly into representation, local residual estimate, and residual-of-residual estimate. Its aggregate is a learned control-variate interface with population variance reduction, while refitting the closer along perturbations of the backbone yields first-order coupled-path mean orthogonality. As an analytical extension, a reflective rumination operator reads the displacement of a global reconstruction from the aggregate anchor, reflects it, and fixes its gain by a unique orthogonal projection rather than return-tuned grid search. On 3.67M Chinese A-share stock-day observations (2023-2026) under a frozen point-in-time protocol, the evaluated base algebra raises net-of-cost return from 13.52% to 19.10% and Sharpe from 1.42 to 2.09. Matched-capacity, unified-residual, identity-free two-stage, and pairwise-only controls all trail it. The gain is therefore not explained by more features or more trees, but by making residual ownership and composition explicit while representation identity is still available.

</details>


### [153] [Trajectory-Relative Hindsight Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07371)
*Haoyu Zheng,Yun Zhu,Qing Wang,Wenqiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRIAL提出了一种轨迹相对的后见之明蒸馏框架，通过统一的对齐打分协议在决策轮次间重新分配密集监督信号，在WebShop和ALFWorld上全面超越GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体强化学习方法利用后见之明来补充稀疏的结果奖励，但一次完成的轨迹可以产生许多这样的信号，如何将这些信号合理地分配到各个决策轮次中是一个尚未解决的问题。

Method: TRIAL为每个决策轮次提取该决策实际后果的结果视图，并在普通与后见条件化的上下文中评估同一响应。通过符号化的对数概率差距确定每个token级监督的方向和局部强度，同时在已实现的轨迹上联合归一化轮次级幅度。分配乘数的合格token加权均值为1，在不改变平均乘数的前提下跨轮次重新分配密集监督。

Result: 在WebShop和ALFWorld上使用不同骨干模型，TRIAL在所有8种（骨干、环境、评估指标）组合中均优于GRPO，并在6种组合中达到六种方法中的最佳或并列最佳性能。在WebShop上使用Qwen3-1.7B时，成功率从56.4%提升至75.2%，任务得分从78.7%提升至85.7%。

Conclusion: 轨迹相对性的轮次分配提供了一种有效的密集后见蒸馏机制，其增益显著超过单纯的密集后见蒸馏，表明跨轮次分配监督信号是提升智能体强化学习性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Recent agentic reinforcement learning methods use hindsight to complement sparse outcome rewards. However, a completed rollout can yield many such signals, leaving their appropriate allocation across turns unclear. We introduce TRIAL, a trajectory-relative hindsight distillation framework with a unified turn-aligned scoring protocol. For each decision turn, TRIAL extracts an outcome view of that decision's realized consequence and evaluates the same response under ordinary and hindsight-conditioned contexts. The signed log-probability gap determines the direction and local strength of token-level supervision, while turn-level magnitudes are normalized jointly over the realized trajectory. The resulting allocation multipliers have an eligible-token-weighted mean of one, redistributing dense supervision across turns while fixing its average multiplier. Experiments on WebShop and ALFWorld with different backbones show that TRIAL outperforms GRPO across all eight combinations of backbone, environment, and evaluation metric, while achieving the best or tied-best performance among six methods on six of them. On WebShop with Qwen3-1.7B, TRIAL improves the success rate from 56.4% to 75.2% and the task score from 78.7% to 85.7%. Controlled ablations further show that trajectory-relative turn allocation provides substantial gains beyond those of dense hindsight distillation alone.

</details>


### [154] [Omni-modal decomposition autoencoders learn full-stack wearable disentangled representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07385)
*Ioannis Ziogas,Ensieh Khazaei,Bilal Taha,Aamna Al Shehhi,Ahsan H. Khandoker,Leontios J. Hadjileontiadis,Dimitrios Hatzinakos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniDecVAEs框架，通过模态条件的时间-频率潜子空间和多视图自监督分解损失，在统一的可扩展模型中同时实现多模态可穿戴计算的任务分类、解耦可解释表示学习、融合与生成建模。在多达三十个模态的HAR场景中，该方法在活动识别和身份识别上分别提升1.01%和6.75%，重建MAE提升76.84%，MMD提升13.85%，且参数量仅为4.1M，适合边缘穿戴设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法未能作为全栈可穿戴处理器运作，即无法同时满足任务分类性能、解耦可解释表示学习、多模态融合以及对高度异构多模态时间序列的生成建模这四项需求。这一缺口限制了通用、多功能且可持续的多模态可穿戴模型的发展。

Method: OmniDecVAEs扩展了DecVAEs架构，通过两个关键设计实现：一是学习模态条件的时间-频率潜子空间，二是采用多视图自监督分解损失及共享非对称自编码器（AE）架构。该框架能够从任意数量的模态中高效、统一且可扩展地学习多用途表示。

Result: 在最多三十个模态的omni-modal HAR基准上，OmniDecVAEs的活动识别准确率提升1.01%，身份识别准确率提升6.75%（对比Transformer和VAE方法）；重建质量MAE改善76.84%；真实与合成数据的分布相似性MMD改善13.85%。模型仅4.1M参数，具有模态不变的参数量规模和实时推理延迟。

Conclusion: OmniDecVAEs展示了作为轻量级全栈可穿戴处理模型的潜力，能够以单一模型统一处理需求与能力，具备增强的表示容量、模态不变的空间复杂度（4.1M参数）和实时延迟，适用于智能边缘穿戴设备和临床医疗保健场景。

Abstract: Learning disentangled representations is a key requirement for developing versatile, general-purpose, and sustainable models in multi-modal wearable computing. However, existing approaches do not operate as full-stack wearable processors, i.e., they do not simultaneously address task-specific classification performance, disentangled and interpretable representation learning, fusion, and generative modeling of highly heterogeneous multi-modal time series. To address this gap, we introduce Omni-modal Variational Decomposition Autoencoders (OmniDecVAEs), a framework that efficiently learns multi-purpose representations in a unified and scalable manner from arbitrarily many modalities. OmniDecVAEs extend DecVAEs by learning modality-conditioned time-frequency latent subspaces through a multi-view self-supervised decomposition loss and a shared asymmetric autoencoder (AE) architecture. Results on a challenging omni-modal human activity recognition (HAR) setting with up to thirty modalities, demonstrate the ability of OmniDecVAEs to learn full-stack wearable representations. When compared to transformer-based and VAE-based methods, OmniDecVAEs full-stack disentangled representation properties lead to accuracy improvements of 1.01% and 6.75% in activity and identity recognition, respectively. Furthermore, OmniDecVAEs synthesize realistic omni-modal time-frequency data that manifest with enhanced reconstructions (mean absolute error improves by 76.84%) and distributional similarity between real and synthetic data (maximum mean discrepancy improves by 13.85%). Our results highlight OmniDecVAEs potential as a lightweight model suitable for intelligent edge wearables and clinical healthcare, unifying processing requirements and abilities in a single model, through its enhanced representational capacity, modality-invariant spatial complexity (4.1M parameters), and real-time latency.

</details>


### [155] [FedDOSE: Federated Learning Framework Decomposing Site Effects for Modeling Brain Dynamic Functional Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07393)
*Deepank Girish,Yi Hao Chan,Yubin Zheng,Sukrit Gupta,Jagath C. Rajapakse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedDOSE，一个用于多站点fMRI动态功能连接（dFC）分析的联邦学习框架，通过模块化引导的Tucker分解编码高维dFC张量，并结合最优传输重心与Procrustes分析对齐类级原型，在ASD和ADHD诊断任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多站点fMRI数据的协作训练面临两大挑战：一是站点差异导致统计异质性，传统联邦学习方法难以应对；二是现有FL方法仅使用静态功能连接，忽略了大脑网络中的动态时变信息。这两个问题限制了多站点fMRI数据分析的准确性和泛化能力，亟需一个能同时解决二者的新框架。

Method: FedDOSE框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 模块化引导的Tucker分解模块，用于对高维dFC张量进行编码，高效捕获模块层面的时空模式；(2) 跨站点类级原型对齐机制，利用最优传输（OT）重心公式与Procrustes分析在全局层面将各站点生成的类级原型对齐，显式分解并处理站点差异。

Result: 在三个多站点静息态fMRI数据集（ABIDE-I、ABIDE-II、ADHD-200）上，针对ASD和ADHD诊断任务的大量实验表明，FedDOSE在性能上超越了现有最先进方法，验证了其从多站点数据中学习鲁棒表示的有效性。

Conclusion: FedDOSE通过显式分解站点差异并利用动态功能连接信息，为多站点fMRI数据的联邦学习提供了一个有效框架，在神经精神疾病诊断中展现出优越的性能和可靠性。

Abstract: Functional Magnetic Resonance Imaging ( fMRI ) data are often pooled into collaborative multi-site consortia, as deep learning models for analyses require large datasets to generalize well. While Federated Learning (FL) offers a privacy-preserving paradigm for collaborative training, standard approaches continue to struggle with statistical heterogeneity. In particular, site differences pose a key challenge in multi-site data settings. Additionally, existing FL approaches for fMRI rely on static Functional Connectivity ( FC), omitting dynamic information in brain networks. To address this, we propose FedDOSE, a novel framework that explicitly decomposes site differences for analysis of dynamic FC (dFC). FedDOSE introduces a Modularity-Guided Tucker Decomposition block to encode high-dimensional dFC tensors and capture modular-level spatio-temporal patterns efficiently. Class-specific prototypes are generated across all sites and subsequently aligned at the global level by using a combination of Optimal Transport (OT) barycenter formulation and Procrustes analysis. Extensive experiments for diagnosing Autism Spectrum Disorder (ASD) and Attention-Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD) on three multi-site resting-state fMRI datasets: ABIDE-I, ABIDE-II, and ADHD-200, demonstrate that FedDOSE outperforms state-of-the-art methods in ASD and ADHD detection. Our results highlight its effectiveness in learning robust representations from multi-site datasets for reliable analysis.

</details>


### [156] [Beyond Post-Hoc Temperature Scaling: Bilevel Optimization for LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07419)
*Ruochen Jin,Zhanliang Wang,Zongyu Dai,Jiancong Xiao,Bojian Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过在训练过程中修改模型参数来改善大语言模型校准性的方法，利用最大化预测分布熵作为校准目标，通过双层优化框架实现，在多项选择和开放式问答任务中取得了良好的校准效果，尤其在跨域泛化方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐通常使LLM过度自信且校准不良；传统的后处理温度缩放是领域依赖的，在某一域拟合的温度无法跨域泛化。因此作者动机是在训练中修改模型参数以提升校准性，而不是依赖后处理。

Method: 提出最大化预测分布熵作为校准目标，直接通过抑制过度集中的预测来对抗过度自信。受温度缩放启发，将其实现为双层优化：下层在参数化损失下训练模型，上层选择损失超参数以最大化熵。为了在LLM规模下可行，采用高效一阶近似，避免显式二阶计算。

Result: 在多项选择和开放式生成式问答任务上的实验表明，该方法使得LLM校准良好，尤其在跨域泛化场景下具有显著优势。

Conclusion: 通过训练阶段的熵最大化校准目标，可以在不依赖领域特定的后处理温度缩放的情况下，获得良好校准且具有更强跨域泛化能力的LLM。

Abstract: Preference alignment often makes large language models (LLMs) overconfident and poorly calibrated. Traditional post-hoc temperature scaling is inherently domain-dependent: a temperature fitted on one domain does not generalize across domains. This motivates us to modify model parameters during training to improve calibration. We propose maximizing the entropy of predictive distributions as the calibration objective, which directly targets overconfidence by discouraging overly concentrated predictions. Inspired by temperature scaling, we realize this through a bilevel optimization formulation, where the lower level trains the model under a parametric loss and the upper level selects loss hyperparameters to maximize entropy. To make the framework practical at LLM scale, we adopt an efficient first-order approximation that avoids explicit second-order computation. Across both multiple-choice and open-ended generative question answering, experiments demonstrate that our method yields well-calibrated LLMs with particular advantages in out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Myopic World Models: Long-Horizon End-to-End Training for Direct Future Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07420)
*Xinyi Li,Zaishuo Xia,Chenjie Hao,Yubei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接预测世界模型（DPWM），通过端到端终点预测目标替代传统的少数步递推训练范式，采用非递归架构将任意长度的动作序列压缩为单一嵌入并单次前向预测终点观测，显著提升了长时域预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型应在长时间范围内支持想象和预测，但现有模型大多使用局部几步预测损失训练，然后递归展开推理。这种范式存在根本性不匹配：少数步损失只优化局部转移保真度，而长时域预测依赖于误差和梯度在整个轨迹上的传播。不同下游影响的转移被同等对待，微小局部误差通过递归推理被放大。

Method: 提出DPWM，一种非递归架构，将任意长度的动作序列压缩为单个嵌入，在单次前向传播中直接预测终点观测。该设计避免递归展开在预测和梯度传播中的使用，使端到端长时域训练在实际可行的范围内稳定进行。

Result: 在连续控制和像素级基准测试中，DPWM在长时域终点预测上显著优于递归世界模型基线，且预测时域越长增益越大。用相同的长时域终点目标重新训练递归基线也能获得类似提升，验证了训练目标而非模型主干是长时域预测精度的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 世界模型应当在最终使用的时间尺度上进行训练和评估，将关注点从局部转移建模转向长时域预测准确性。训练目标的选择是提升长时域预测精度的关键，而非特定模型架构。

Abstract: World models are expected to support imagination over extended temporal horizons, yet most are still trained through local few-step prediction objectives and deployed by recursively rolling out their own predictions. This creates a fundamental mismatch: few-step losses optimize local transition fidelity, while long-horizon prediction depends on how errors and gradients propagate through the entire trajectory. As a result, transitions with different downstream influence on the endpoint are treated uniformly during training, and small local errors are amplified through recursive inference. We argue that long-horizon accuracy is better achieved by optimizing directly, through an end-to-end endpoint prediction objective. To instantiate this paradigm, we introduce the Direct Prediction World Model (DPWM), a non-recursive architecture that compresses an action sequence of arbitrary length into a single embedding and predicts the endpoint observation in a single forward pass. This design avoids recurrent rollout in both prediction and gradient propagation, making long-horizon end-to-end training practical at horizons where unrolled autoregressive training becomes unstable. Empirically, DPWM substantially improves long-horizon endpoint prediction over recursive world-model baselines on continuous-control and pixel-based benchmarks, with larger gains as the prediction horizon increases. We further show that recurrent baselines benefit similarly when retrained with the same long-horizon endpoint objective, supporting our central claim that the training objective, rather than the particular backbone choice, is the main driver of long-horizon prediction accuracy. Our results suggest that world models can benefit from being trained and evaluated at the temporal scales where they are ultimately used, shifting the focus from local transition modeling toward long-horizon predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [158] [Diffusion LLMs as Targets and Adversaries: Mechanistic Safety Exploits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07430)
*Elena Dumitrescu,Gert Lek,Lydia Y. Chen,Jérémie Decouchant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散大语言模型（DLLMs）的内部安全机制，发现其安全对齐稀疏且跨架构可迁移。作者提出SN-Guided Diffusion，一个完全离线的黑盒越狱框架，通过加权安全神经元损失引导扩散过程远离安全触发区域，在多个目标上实现高达86.9%的攻击成功率，且仅需每提示20次生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型用迭代并行去噪替代自回归自回归式逐token预测，但其内部安全机制尚未被充分理解。既有研究缺乏对DLLMs安全对齐机制的深入分析，也无法判断其是否存在可被利用的脆弱性，亟需系统性地研究DLLMs本身及其作为攻击者的安全机制弱点。

Method: 首先验证DLLMs的安全对齐是稀疏且可迁移的：DLLMs继承自其自回归前身的安全机制足迹，可直接通过安全神经元映射和剪枝实现迁移攻击。在此基础上提出了SN-Guided Diffusion框架，利用加权安全神经元损失在完全离线的黑盒场景中引导扩散过程远离安全触发区域，以实现越狱攻击。

Result: 自剪枝将LLaDA的攻击成功率从2.6%提升至73.8%，Dream从1.9%提升至86.6%。跨模型迁移剪枝在Dream上从1.9%提升至73.2%，在Fast-dLLM上从7.0%提升至86.3%。SN-Guided Diffusion实现了近乎完美的提示可分性（AUROC=1.0），在多个开放和专有模型上达到77.1%（Llama-3-8B-Instruct）、86.9%（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）和74.3%（Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite）的迁移攻击成功率，每提示仅需20次生成迭代。

Conclusion: DLLMs的安全对齐在机制层面存在根本性脆弱性——稀疏且易迁移，安全机制继承自源自回归模型导致可被剪枝和映射攻击利用。SN-Guided Diffusion证明了在低生成成本下即可实现高效黑盒越狱，揭示了扩散式对齐在安全性上的严重局限。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (DLLMs) replace autoregressive next-token prediction with iterative parallel denoising, yet their internal safety mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we investigate DLLMs both as targets and as adversaries, exposing mechanistic vulnerabilities in diffusion-based alignment.
  We first show that safety alignment in DLLMs remains sparse and transferable across architectures. DLLMs initialized from autoregressive predecessors inherit the same mechanistic safety footprint as their source models, enabling transfer attacks via direct safety neuron mapping and pruning. Self-pruning increases attack success rates (ASR) from 2.6% to 73.8% on LLaDA and from 1.9% to 86.6% on Dream, while transfer pruning from Qwen2.5 increases ASR from 1.9% to 73.2% on Dream and from 7.0% to 86.3% on Fast-dLLM.
  Building on these findings, we introduce SN-Guided Diffusion, a fully offline black-box jailbreak framework that steers the diffusion process away from safety-triggering regions using a weighted safety neuron loss, which achieves near-perfect prompt separability (AUROC = 1.0 for benign-vs-jailbreak discrimination). Across multiple open and proprietary targets, our method achieves a transfer ASR of up to 77.1% on Llama-3-8B-Instruct, 86.9% on Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and 74.3% against Gemini-2.5-Flash-Lite, while requiring only 20 generation episodes per prompt. Compared to prior jailbreaking frameworks, our method achieves competitive transferability with orders-of-magnitude lower generation cost.
  Our codebase is available at https://github.com/ellyoana/sn-guided-diffusion.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [159] [A Low-Latency ASIC Architecture for Real-Time Line Segment Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06439)
*Amir Hossein Jalilvand,Parsa Hassani Shariat Panahi,M. Hassan Najafi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于步长算法的低延迟ASIC架构，用于实时线段检测。该设计采用全流水线架构，每时钟周期处理一个像素，具有确定性延迟。在45nm CMOS工艺下，VGA分辨率下达到325 FPS，Full HD下48 FPS，功耗25.54 mW，面积0.412 mm²。相比基于Line Hough Transform的90nm ASIC实现，功耗降低49%，帧率提升1.6倍以上，适用于边缘计算场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线段检测是嵌入式视觉（如自主导航、视觉SLAM、工业检测）的关键预处理步骤。深度学习方法精度高但资源消耗大，难以部署在资源受限平台；经典算法效率高但延迟与内容相关，缺乏确定性。因此需要一种低延迟、低功耗、小面积的硬件加速方案。

Method: 提出五种ASIC专用设计特性：1) 基于寄存器的行缓冲与数据复用；2) 无乘法器MCM（Multiple Constant Multiplication）滤波；3) 8类角度量化；4) 类似CAM的关联存储器实现单周期匹配；5) 优化的重复去除机制。整体采用全流水线架构，基于步长（step-length）算法实现每周期处理一个像素的确定性延迟。

Result: 在45nm CMOS工艺下综合实现：VGA分辨率325 FPS、Full HD 48 FPS，功耗25.54 mW，面积0.412 mm²；125 MHz时钟下VGA分辨率可达406 FPS，功耗31.48 mW。与90nm LHT ASIC实现相比，功耗降低49%，帧率提升超过1.6倍。

Conclusion: 该架构在实时性能、低功耗和小面积需求之间取得了良好平衡，非常适合边缘计算场景中要求实时线段检测的应用，为资源受限平台上的线段检测提供了高效的硬件加速解决方案。

Abstract: Line segment detection is a critical preprocessing step in embedded vision applications such as autonomous navigation, visual SLAM, and industrial inspection. Deep learning methods achieve high accuracy but require substantial resources, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained platforms. Classical algorithms are efficient but exhibit content-dependent latency. This paper presents a low-latency ASIC architecture for real-time line segment detection. The proposed design is based on the step-length algorithm and incorporates five ASIC-specific features: register-based line buffering with data reuse, multiplierless MCM-based filtering, 8-class angle quantization, a CAM-like associative memory for single-cycle matching, and an optimized duplicate removal mechanism. The architecture is fully pipelined and processes one pixel per clock cycle with deterministic latency. Synthesized in a 45nm CMOS process, the design achieves 325 FPS at VGA resolution and 48 FPS at Full HD, with 25.54 mW power consumption and 0.412 mm\textsuperscript{2} area. At 125 MHz, the throughput increases to 406 FPS at VGA resolution with 31.48 mW power consumption. Compared with a 90nm ASIC implementation based on the Line Hough Transform, the proposed design reduces power consumption by 49\% and delivers over 1.6 times higher frame rate. The architecture is well suited for edge-computing applications requiring real-time performance, low power, and minimal area.

</details>


### [160] [Retention-Aware RISC-V ISA Extension and Memory Controller on FPGA for MLC NVM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06725)
*Mina Ibrahim,Martel Shokry,Lokesh Siddhu,Lars Bauer,Hassan Nassar,Joerg Henkel*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对MLC NVM优化的内存控制器架构和RISC-V指令集扩展，利用MLC NVM在写延迟与保留时间之间的权衡，通过位重要性感知写入（关键位用高保留写入，非关键位用低保留快写）以及fast-store指令来提升写性能，并在FPGA上完成了硬件原型验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLC NVM技术在提高内存密度方面潜力巨大，其核心特点是在写延迟与保留时间之间存在权衡（快速写入保留时间短，慢速写入保留时间长）。然而，目前缺乏在系统层面利用这一权衡、并在硬件上验证和原型化的相关工作，这是本文要填补的研究空白。

Method: 本文提出了三项关键技术方案：(1) 基于有限状态机的自定义NVM内存控制器，采用AXI内存映射接口并支持增强的突发传输以降低延迟；(2) 在RISC-V指令集中引入fast-store指令以提高写性能同时解决保留时间限制；(3) 设计专用的AXI从设备外设，支持位重要性感知写入：关键位（如MSB）使用慢速高保留写入，非关键位（如LSB）使用快速低保留写入，在不牺牲数据可靠性的前提下提升性能。所有增强均在FPGA平台上以硬件实现。

Result: 实验结果表明：该控制器相比传统设计减少了30%的硬件开销；fast-store指令在流式工作负载下性能提升超过7%，且硬件开销低于0.08%；位级AXI外设在64x64矩阵下LUT利用率低于3.5%，在32x32尺寸下低于1%，适合集成到更大规模的SoC中。

Conclusion: 本文通过硬件实现验证了在系统层面利用MLC NVM写延迟-保留时间权衡的可行性。提出的控制器架构、fast-store指令和位重要性感知写入机制在性能提升与硬件开销之间取得了良好平衡，为NVM系统在真实硬件中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Non-volatile memory (NVM) technologies, particularly Multi-Level Cell (MLC) NVMs, offer significant potential for increasing memory density. MLC NVMs provide a tradeoff between write latency and retention time, where faster writes/stores result in lower retention and slower writes yield higher retention. However, limited work has been done to validate and prototype NVM-based systems in hardware, leveraging this tradeoff at the system level. In this paper, we present a novel memory controller architecture and a RISC-V instruction set extension to optimize MLC NVM write operations by balancing speed and retention time. Our custom NVM controller, built around a finite state machine with an AXI memory-mapped interface, efficiently manages read/write operations with enhanced burst transfers, minimizing latency. Additionally, we introduce a fast-store instruction in RISC-V to increasing write performance while addressing retention limitations. Further, we design a dedicated AXI slave peripheral that supports bit-significance-aware writes: critical bits (e.g., MSBs) are written using slower, high-retention writes, while non-critical bits (e.g., LSBs) use faster, low-retention writes to help enhance performance without compromising data reliability. These enhancements are implemented in hardware on an FPGA platform. Experimental results show that our controller reduces hardware overhead by 30% compared to conventional designs, and the fast-store instruction improves performance by over 7% for streaming workloads with less than 0.08% hardware overhead. The bit-wise AXI peripheral has a LUT utilization staying below 3.5% even for 64x64 matrices, and under 1% for 32x32 sizes, making it viable for integration into larger SoCs.

</details>


### [161] [HLSmith: An Expert-Guided Agentic Framework for C/C++-to-HLS Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06791)
*Yuebo Luo,Ahmad Sedigh Baroughi,Philip Stachura,Le Chen,Venkatram Vishwanath,Zhenman Fang,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HLSmith是一个专家引导的框架，用于将C/C++程序自动翻译为优化的高综合（HLS）FPGA加速器设计。它结合了HLS优化专家库、分阶段反馈驱动的编排流程和工具落地的模型适配管道，在PolyBench基准上相比现有框架ChatHLS实现了4.24倍的几何平均加速，并达到100%的设计有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用特定的FPGA加速器能带来显著的性能和能效优势，但开发成本高昂，即使使用高综合（HLS）也需要大量硬件专业知识。虽然大型语言模型（LLM）在软件生成方面表现强劲，但即便是前沿模型也缺乏硬件直觉和程序性知识，无法可靠地将基线C/C++程序转换为高性能HLS设计，尤其在识别有效架构、遵循专家优化流程和跨多样内核一致应用硬件变换方面存在显著不足。

Method: HLSmith包含三个核心组件：(1) HLS优化专业知识库，编码带守卫的变换配方、适用性与前提条件以及不安全案例；(2) 分阶段、反馈驱动的编排流程，模拟专家HLS开发实践，引导智能体完成综合、瓶颈分析和优化；(3) 工具落地的模型适配管道，将商业前沿模型的优化轨迹转化为开放权重LLM的微调训练数据。

Result: 在PolyBench基准上的评估中，HLSmith相比领先的先前框架ChatHLS实现了4.24倍的几何平均加速，且所有基准在软件和RTL仿真中均功能正确，而ChatHLS仅有57%的有效设计率。使用商业前沿模型时达到252倍加速，使用开放权重模型时达到138倍加速。

Conclusion: HLSmith通过将专家HLS优化知识编码、模拟专家开发流程的编排机制，以及模型适配管道相结合，有效弥合了LLM在硬件设计领域的能力差距，实现了可靠的自动化HLS加速器生成，在加速效果和设计有效性上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Application-specific FPGA accelerators offer substantial performance and energy-efficiency gains across many application domains, but developing them is costly, often requiring months of specialized effort. Even with high-level synthesis (HLS), designers still need extensive hardware expertise to build high-performance accelerators. Although large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong software-generation capabilities, even frontier models lack the hardware intuition and procedural knowledge needed to reliably translate baseline C/C++ programs into high-performance HLS designs: they struggle to identify effective architectures, follow the optimization processes used by HLS experts, and apply hardware transformations consistently across diverse kernels. We present HLSmith, an expert-guided framework for translating C/C++ programs into optimized HLS accelerators. HLSmith combines three components: an HLS optimization expertise library that encodes guarded transformation recipes, their applicability and prerequisite conditions, and unsafe cases to avoid; a staged, feedback-driven orchestration flow modeled on expert HLS development practice that guides agents through synthesis, bottleneck analysis, and optimization; and a tool-grounded model-adaptation pipeline that converts optimization trajectories from commercial frontier models into training data for fine-tuning open-weight LLMs. We evaluate HLSmith on PolyBench against ChatHLS, a leading prior agent-orchestration framework for HLS accelerator development. HLSmith achieves a geometric mean speedup of 4.24x over ChatHLS while producing functionally correct designs, in both software and RTL simulation, for every benchmark, compared with ChatHLS's 57% valid-design rate. It further reaches speedups of up to 252x and 138x with commercial frontier models and open-weight models, respectively.

</details>


### [162] [G-Power: Architecture-level GPU Power Modeling with Aggregated Knowledge Foundations from Known GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06870)
*Qijun Zhang,Yao Lu,Shang Liu,Mengming Li,Chen Zhang,Dongbo Wang,Zhiyao Xie*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出G-Power，一种基于迁移学习的架构级GPU功耗建模框架。通过利用额外已知GPU芯片的知识作为基础，对目标GPU进行微调，解决了现有模型因仅使用微基准测试数据训练而导致精度低的问题。在四款现代NVIDIA GPU上评估，平均MAPE低至14%，相关系数R达0.88，相比AccelWattch分别改善22%的MAPE和0.36的R。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GPU功耗优化依赖于快速的架构级功耗评估，然而现有功耗模型如AccelWattch由于功耗标签收集耗时，只能使用简单的微基准测试进行训练，训练数据的局限性导致模型精度较低。

Method: G-Power采用三阶段算法：1）利用额外已知GPU芯片进行预训练，建立聚合知识基础；2）采用注意力启发的聚合机制以捕获相似性；3）在目标GPU上进行微调。

Result: 在四款现代NVIDIA GPU上评估，G-Power平均MAPE为14%，平均相关系数R为0.88，相比AccelWattch，MAPE降低22%，R提高0.36。

Conclusion: G-Power通过迁移学习利用已知GPU芯片知识，有效克服了训练数据不足的限制，显著提升了架构级GPU功耗模型的精度。

Abstract: Graphics Processing Units (GPUs) have been serving as critical computation resources for large-scale parallel computations. With increasing chip complexity, power efficiency has become an important design objective for modern GPUs. GPU power optimization relies on fast power evaluation, requiring architecture-level GPU power model. However, because of the time-consuming power label collection, only simple microbenchmarks are adopted for training. The limitation of microbenchmarks as training data incurs low accuracy for existing architecture-level GPU power models like AccelWattch.
  To address the limitation of microbenchmarks as training data, we propose G-Power, an architecture-level GPU power modeling framework that utilizes additional known GPU chips to provide additional knowledge. G-Power utilizes the aggregated knowledge foundation from additional known GPU chips and then performs fine-tuning on our target GPU. To provide foundations with additional known GPU chips and capture the similarity to utilize these foundations for fine-tuning, G-Power adopts a three-phase algorithm consisting of 1) pre-training with additional known chips, 2) attention-inspired aggregation, and 3) fine-tuning on our target GPU. We evaluate G-Power on four modern NVIDIA GPUs, demonstrating high accuracy. G-Power can achieve a low MAPE of 14% and a high correlation coefficient R of 0.88 on average, which are 22% lower MAPE and 0.36 higher R than AccelWattch.

</details>


### [163] [Rethinking Unified Memory for NPU-PIM Systems: Dual-View Memory for Dynamic Inference of LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06989)
*Shixin Zhao,Lian Liu,Tianhua Han,Mengdi Wang,Yinhe Han,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PFM是一个面向NPU-PIM异构架构的双视图内存系统，通过解耦物理数据布局与访问者可见的逻辑视图，实现跨设备高效数据访问，无需数据复制或重布局，从而支持LLM推理中动态变化的执行设备需求，端到端吞吐量提升最高达2.32倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NPU-PIM统一内存设计假设每个张量绑定固定执行设备，采用静态、设备偏向的数据映射。然而现代LLM工作负载中，由于阶段切换（如prefill vs. decode）和动态行为（如MoE路由），同一张量的最优执行设备会在运行时发生变化，导致设备偏向映射与访问模式不匹配，造成带宽利用不足和性能损失。

Method: PFM采用双视图内存系统：将物理数据存储于联合优化的布局中，同时为NPU和PIM提供不同的逻辑视图，实现跨设备高效访问而不需要数据复制或重布局；并设计了访问者感知的地址翻译和运行时调度机制，以支持LLM工作负载波动时动态执行设备的灵活切换。

Result: 在多种LLM上的评估表明，PFM将端到端吞吐量提升最高达2.32倍，展示了其作为NPU-PIM系统统一内存管理方案的有效性和广泛适用性。

Conclusion: PFM通过双视图内存设计有效解决了NPU-PIM异构架构中动态执行场景下的设备偏向数据映射问题，在不引入数据复制和重布局开销的前提下实现了跨设备的高效数据访问，为异构LLM推理加速提供了统一内存管理解决方案。

Abstract: Heterogeneous architectures that combine neural processing unit (NPU) and processing-in-memory (PIM) are increasingly adopted to accelerate LLM inference. Prior work focuses on building a unified memory that allows NPUs and PIM to share data without duplication. However, these designs implicitly assume that each tensor is bound to a fixed execution device, and therefore rely on static, device-biased data mappings.
  We observe that this assumption does not hold in modern LLM workloads. Due to phase changes (e.g., prefill vs. decode) and dynamic behaviors such as MoE routing, the optimal execution device for the same tensor can change at runtime. Under such dynamic execution, device-biased mappings become mismatched to access patterns, leading to substantial bandwidth underutilization and performance loss. This paper presents PFM (PIM-as-Flexible-Memory), a dual-view memory system that decouples physical data layout from accessor-visible logical views. PFM stores data in a jointly optimized physical layout and exposes different logical interpretations to NPUs and PIM, enabling efficient access across devices without data duplication or relayout. We further design accessor-aware address translation and runtime scheduling mechanisms to support dynamic execution when LLM workloads fluctuate and the optimal execution device dynamically changes. Our evaluation across LLMs shows that PFM improves end-to-end throughput by up to 2.32$\times$, demonstrating its effectiveness and broad applicability as a unified memory management solution for NPU-PIM systems.

</details>


### [164] [Dual-Node NVIDIA DGX Spark over Tailscale: A Remote-Access Testbed for Distributed LLM Training and Cyber-Threat-Intelligence Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07226)
*Vasanth Iyer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项在双节点NVIDIA DGX Spark系统上分布式预训练NanoChat模型的概念验证部署，通过200Gb/s QSFP56光纤直连链路，实现了约1890 tokens/s的训练速度，并在四天内处理了约6.53亿个token。同时构建了基于77份CISA安全公告的网络安全微调数据集并进行评估，证明了小型实验室可在桌面级硬件上进行多节点训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧凑型AI系统使本地语言模型实验日益普及，但桌面级加速器上多节点训练的实际证据仍然有限。作者旨在填补这一空白，通过在两个DGX Spark系统上部署分布式训练，为小型实验室提供可复现的多节点训练参考。

Method: 使用PyTorch torchrun、DDP和NCCL配置分布式训练，每节点单进程，采用深度20的NanoChat模型，本地批大小32、上下文2048 token。节点间通过Tailscale mesh VPN管理并配备专用200Gb/s QSFP56直连光纤链路。另基于77份CISA公告构建网络安全微调数据集（338训练/37验证对话），使用Ollama托管的LLM judge进行17道保留问题的评估。

Result: 训练稳定维持在约69.4秒/步（约1890 tokens/s），四天处理约6.53亿token。CTI增强检查点在威胁情报类别上表现提升但一般知识类别退步，总体评分从2.06微升至2.29（0-10分制）。该集群同时支持CS 426课程和CompTIA Security+查询引擎。

Conclusion: 研究确立了桌面级多节点训练的可行性而非扩展效率声明（因单节点吞吐量是估算而非在匹配条件下实测）。作者提供了完整的运行手册、配置细节和排障经验，作为小型实验室的可复现性参考。

Abstract: Compact AI systems make local language-model experimentation increasingly accessible, yet practical evidence for multi-node training on desktop-class accelerators remains limited. This report presents a proof-of-concept deployment of distributed NanoChat pretraining across two NVIDIA DGX Spark systems, each with a GB10 Grace Blackwell system-on-chip and 128 GB of unified memory, administered remotely over a Tailscale mesh VPN and connected for training by a dedicated 200 Gb/s QSFP56 direct fiber link. PyTorch torchrun, DDP, and NCCL were configured with one process per node, a depth-20 NanoChat model, a local batch size of 32 per node, and a 2,048-token context, giving a global batch of 131,072 tokens per step. The run sustained a step time of about 69.4 s (about 1,890 tokens/s), processing about 653 million tokens over four days. We document link configuration, container setup, interface binding, a step-zero evaluation bug that triggered NCCL timeouts, checkpointing, and troubleshooting lessons, as a reproducibility reference for small labs.
  We also built a cybersecurity fine-tuning dataset from 77 CISA advisories (338 training, 37 validation conversations) and ran a 17-question held-out evaluation comparing a baseline SFT checkpoint against a CTI-augmented checkpoint with an Ollama-hosted LLM judge. CTI-specific categories improved while general-knowledge categories regressed, for a small overall change from 2.06 to 2.29 on a 0-10 scale. The same cluster supports a 400-level AI course (CS 426) and a query engine for CompTIA Security+ POGIL activities in CBS 255, showing modest local infrastructure can serve both research and teaching. The study establishes feasibility rather than a scaling-efficiency claim, since single-node throughput used for comparison was estimated, not measured under matched conditions. Runbook and scripts are available (see Code Availability).

</details>
